<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
This is a thorough Phase 3 analysis. Let me do a formal review:

**Positive observations:**
1. Well-structured with data inventory showing precision per α level
2. BH FDR correction properly applied
3. Raw vs FDR comparison shown
4. Phase 2 vs 3 comparison showing multi-seed stability effects
5. α=0.60 bf16 caveat explicitly noted (#6)

**Review concerns:**

1. **α=0.60 is the only bf16 data point in an otherwise fp32 panel** - This is a major consistency issue. The Phase 3 report already notes this (finding #6), but the implication is stronger than stated: you CANNOT meaningfully compare α=0.60's detection rates to neighboring α values (α=0.50 fp32, α=0.75 fp32) because D079 established that bf16 suppresses collapse 5-730×. 

The α=0.60 "step function" jump in permG could be entirely an artifact of precision difference, not a real dose-response effect.

2. **naive at 96% for α=0.00 (null control)** - This is correctly flagged as spurious, but the FPR should be 0% at null by definition. 96% FPR means naive_xcorr is fundamentally broken as a test. Good that the paper treats it as a foil, but this should be front-and-center in the method validation narrative.

3. **diff and threshold have non-zero FDR-corrected rates at α=0.00** - This suggests these methods are also detecting spurious signal in the null case, which undermines their reliability as controls.

4. **α=0.50 permG: 50% raw → 0% FDR (12 flips)** - This is the critical boundary. The clustering of p-values near the FDR threshold suggests genuine signal that's borderline detectable. Increasing sample size (more seeds) at α=0.50 could tip this either way.

5. **Multi-seed instability**: α=0.25 diff changed from 25% to 62% just by adding one seed. This suggests individual seed results are unreliable and single-seed data points should be heavily caveated.

6. **Phase 2 → Phase 3 changes are** - I need to see what's happening across these phases to understand the broader pattern.

The instability between phases is striking: threshold FPR dropped from 62% to 46% at α=0.00, and α=0.25 TY went from 38% to 0% with just a couple additional seeds. This volatility suggests my current estimates with only 2-3 seeds are likely unreliable themselves—I'd need proper bootstrap confidence intervals to assess how much these numbers might shift with more data.

For the paper, the dose-response map is supposed to be the main evidence for the sensitivity analysis claim, but the mixed precision formats (α=0.60 using bf16 while others use fp32) create a confounding factor that weakens that interpretation. I also need to explicitly flag the non-zero false positive rates for diff and threshold at the null as a limitation. permG looks like the most reliable method overall—it stays at 0% at null and shows good sensitivity at high α values—but its step-function behavior makes it more of a binary classifier than a graded measure.

Looking more carefully at the raw numbers: permG at α=0.00 shows 12% raw significance that drops to 0% after FDR correction, meaning 3 out of 24 tests were significant at null. For a permutation Granger test, that's actually close to the expected 5% nominal rate when accounting for multiple testing, so the FDR correction is working properly and permG's behavior is sound. For diff, I'm seeing 50% raw significance collapse to 33% after FDR, which represents 12 raw significant tests getting filtered down to 8—a much larger raw FPR that the correction handles, but still worth noting.

The threshold method shows similar inflation at 46% raw significance, and both diff and threshold have problematically high false positive rates at null that should be flagged in the review, even though the Phase 3 analysis already correctly identifies the method hierarchy and the team is aware of these issues.

I need to prioritize writing up the D061 and Phase 3 method ablation review, check on the scale_14b completion status, and track the LinAlgError fix progress.
```

```text
Results:
- scale_14b gen_10: empty directory (total 0) → still in early gen_10 processing. But wait, gen_10 directory exists but is empty. This could mean it's starting gen_10 (creating directory, hasn't generated data yet), or it could mean there's an issue.

- westd-19828 processes:
  - PID 369475: GPU-2f112... → 37006 MiB (cuda:0, scale_14b)
  - PID 395612: GPU-11e1... → 37832 MiB (cuda:1, SmolLM3)
  - PID 399353: GPU-11e1... → 37598 MiB (cuda:1, SmolLM3)

I'm noticing something odd here—there are two processes running on the same GPU (GPU-11e1e6d6), both supposedly SmolLM3, consuming a combined 75430 MiB on cuda:1. That's a lot of memory for a 3B model, so I need to figure out what's actually happening there.

The total memory usage adds up to about 75430 MiB out of 97887 MiB available, which is still within limits, but having two Python processes on the same GPU is suspicious. This could mean a parent-child multiprocessing setup, a leftover process from a crash, or intentional multi-process usage. What's throwing me off is that the earlier nvidia-smi output showed only 57138 MiB total for GPU 1, but the per-process breakdown shows 75430 MiB—there's a discrepancy I need to account for, possibly from shared memory or timing differences between the queries.

I should check whether one of these processes is an orphaned SmolLM3 v1 instance that wasn't properly cleaned up when it was killed, since the registry shows both a killed v1 run and a currently running v2 run.
```

Consistent temporal precedence (cross-correlation lag=+1, r=0.80–0.94) is observed across all contamination levels, though formal Granger tests at α≤0.50 do not reach FDR-corrected significance at T=11 (power analysis: §X).\\\"*升级上限：Granger ≠ true causality（Granger 1969），Discussion 一句话声明；双变量模型无法排除未观测混淆因子（详见 Reviewer Concerns 表）。ECE 前向 consensus 与 token_entropy 等效但存在 bidirectional coupling（详见约束 #9）。注意：TY Granger 在 N=11 下不可用于区分真/伪关联（见 Claim 3），核心证据链基于多方法一致性而非单一 TY 背书。**D063 泛化证据**：Table 7 跨 7 conditions（3 architectures × 2 scales × 2 domains）K≥4=5/8-6/8。**Diversity-only subset（排除→ppl pairs）consensus 41/42 = 97.6%**，仅 1.4B s42 的 1 个 diversity pair 未达 K≥4。→ppl pairs 0/14 达 K≥4（结构性失败，非方法缺陷），canary→perplexity 的系统性弱信号作为 explicit finding 报告——diversity collapse 是可预测的下游维度，perplexity 不是。\\n2. **Regime-Dependent Predictability**: 可预测性取决于 collapse 维度和 contamination regime——canary→diversity 在差分后仍 robust（6/6 pair × 4/4 seed），但 canary→perplexity **经差分检验确认为伪回归**（0/4 seed, F≈0, p=0.99），未差分时的\\\"信号\\\"（F=16-36）完全由共享非平稳趋势驱动（D017）。ECE→perplexity 的反向因果同理存疑。**Claim 2 reframing (D022)**: 从\\\"不同 α 下 lead time 不同\\\"升级为\\\"不同 α 下 **detectability** 不同\\\"——α=0.25 差分 Granger 0/8 FDR sig vs α=1.0 的 24/24，同一 pair 的统计可检测性随 contamination level 剧变。**D037 更新**：5-point regime map 完整，detectability 在 α=0.50→0.75 间出现阶跃（0/8→3/8 FDR-sig），α=0.75 consensus 11/16 robust。**Evidence strategy (Reviewer 共识)**：Primary = raw drift magnitude dose-response（distinct_1: -14%@α=0 → -40%@α=0.25 → ~-93%@α=1.0），方法无关、不可争议；Secondary = 差分 Granger FDR sig rate vs α，展示 detectability boundary at α~0.75。**不用 naive_xcorr 做 primary**——与 Claim 3 自相矛盾。Predictability regime map 划定可预测/不可预测边界（\\\"phase diagram\\\" 需 ≥6 个 α 值 + uncertainty interval 才成立，当前 5 个 α 值下用 \\\"regime map\\\"）。\\n3. **Method Selection Sensitivity and Regime-Dependent Validity**（升级自 Methodological Rigor；D022 升级候选：method × regime interaction）: 在典型 generation 数（N≈10）下，lead-lag 结论对分析方法和超参数高度敏感，且**同一方法在不同 α regime 下结论翻转**（diff Granger: α=1.0 24/24 FDR sig → α=0.25 0/8）。升级措辞候选：\\\"Method sensitivity is compounded by regime dependence: the same statistical test applied to the same metric pair can yield significant or null results depending on contamination level\\\"。**三层证据结构**：**(Layer 1)** naive Pearson 找到 16/16 显著——方法宽松时一切 \\\"显著\\\"；**(Layer 2)** 一阶差分翻转整条 perplexity 通道（8/8→0/8 SIG）而 diversity 通道不变（12/12→12/12）——一个方法论选择决定通道存亡（D017）；**(Layer 3)** TY d_max sensitivity 使结论在 ~0%↔94% spurious rate 间震荡——超参数选择同样致命。方法论贡献不是\\\"提供正确的检验\\\"，而是**暴露方法选择敏感性并提出多方法一致性框架**——只有跨方法（cross-corr + 差分 + threshold + permutation Granger）和跨 seed 都一致的关联才可信。这是迭代训练文献中被忽视的方法论陷阱。\\n\\n## Story Spine (D027: Option B reframe)\\n\\n- **Problem**: Model collapse 文献中的 lead-lag 分析（\\\"哪些指标预测崩溃\\\"）日益增多（Du et al., SIGMA, DCES），但**方法选择对结论的影响未被系统性审视**。Du et al. 非正式观察到 ECE 领先 accuracy，仅限单指标对、1-3 代、无统计检验。现有工作的方法论基础薄弱：无差分检验（不排除伪回归）、无多方法交叉验证、无多重比较校正、无 regime-dependent 分析。\\n\\n- **Gap**: 没有工作系统性回答：(1) lead-lag 结论在多大程度上取决于分析方法的选择？(2) 同一统计检验在不同 contamination regime 下结论是否稳定？(3) 如何建立可靠的 lead-lag 判定框架？**Scheffer et al. (2009, 2012)** 在生态/气候科学中建立了临界转变早期预警框架，但尚未被 model collapse 文献引入，其方法论严谨性（多方法交叉验证 + regime-dependent analysis）恰好是本领域所缺失的。\\n\\n- **Our approach**: 以 canary prediction for model collapse 为系统性 case study，暴露 lead-lag 分析中的三个方法论陷阱：(1) 方法选择敏感性——同一数据集上 5 种统计方法从 100% 显著到 0% 显著（Claim 3）；(2) 未差分的伪回归——canary→perplexity 通道在差分后完全消失（Claim 1 case study）；(3) regime-dependent validity——同一检验在不同 α 下结论翻转（Claim 2）。在此基础上提出**多方法一致性共识框架**（multi-method consensus framework）作为解决方案：只有跨方法（cross-corr + 差分 + threshold + permutation Granger）和跨 seed 都一致的关联才可信。\\n\\n- **Headline result (D037 更新)**: \\\"In iterative synthetic training with Pythia-410M, naive correlation identifies 100% of canary-collapse metric pairs as significant, while differenced permutation Granger retains only 25% — and this method-dependent gap is compounded by regime dependence: the same test applied to the same metric pair yields 24/24 FDR-significant results at α=1.0, 3/4 at α=0.75, but 0/8 at α≤0.50. We propose a multi-method consensus framework and, applying it, identify token entropy as a robust 1-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse at α≥0.75.\\\"\\n\\n- **Title direction**: \\\"Method Matters: Pitfalls and a Multi-Method Consensus Framework for Lead-Lag Analysis in Iterative Training Collapse\\\"\\n\\n- **文献定位 (保留)**：SIGMA 提供谱监控但无 lead-time 量化；DCES 反应式而非预测式；Du et al. 是最接近的经验前辈但方法论不足；Kalinowski 拓扑 CI 针对表征坍缩非合成数据退化且计算昂贵。Truong & Truong 证明一阶相变下标准早期预警指标失效。理论分析方面：Seddik et al. (2024), Gerstgrasser et al. (2024), Dey et al. (2024) 提供互补理论视角但不涉及方法论敏感性。**我们与 Du et al. 的核心区别**：(1) 多指标家族系统对比；(2) 多方法交叉验证 + d_max sensitivity；(3) regime-dependent mapping；(4) **方法选择敏感性本身是主要贡献**（不再是\\\"独立贡献\\\"而是\\\"核心贡献\\\"）。\\n\\n## Anticipated | Discussion 声明 + future work |\\n\\n## Venue Fit\\n\\nEMNLP 2026 Main Track 优势：\\n- NLP 社区对 model collapse / data contamination 高度关注，audience 精准\\n- ACL/EMNLP 接受 empirical methodology + analysis seed, F≈0, p=0.99, D017），未差分时的\\\"弱信号\\\"完全由共享非平稳趋势驱动。论文措辞用\\\"spurious association confirmed by first-differencing\\\"，不再用\\\"弱信号\\\"\\n2. **ECE→perplexity 和 token_entropy→perplexity 均为伪信号 (D017)** — 差分后两者均 0/4 seed 显著。未差分时观察到的\\\"方向相反\\\"（token_entropy 领先 vs perplexity 领先 ECE）是共享趋势的不同表现形式。论文报告差分前后对比作为方法论教训，不再需要机制性解释\\n3. **effective_rank 和 self_bleu 作为 canary 失败** — 必须全量报告，不能只报正面结果\\n4. **distinct_3 必须放 supplementary，不能完全省略** — max_lag=7 下升为 SIGNIFICANT 但 lag_std>1.5（双峰：seed 42 总是 -5，其余 -1/-2），维持 D008 降级策略，主文聚焦 distinct_1/2。Supplementary 需给完整结果 + 解释 lag instability（高阶 n-gram diversity 对短序列随机变异更敏感）。distinct_3 的不稳定性本身支持 \\\"predictability boundary\\\" 主题——即使 diversity 维度内部也有可预测性梯度\\n5. **max_lag=7 确认核心 lead 无截断** — token_entropy→distinct_1 lag=-1（std=0.0）、ECE→distinct_2 lag=-1（std=0.0）在 max_lag=5/7 下完全一致。token_entropy→perplexity lag=-5 虽非截断伪影，但已确认为非平稳伪回归（D017）\\n6. **~~Dual-timescale 升级条件~~ — 已失效 (D017)** — perplexity 通道经差分确认为伪回归，即使 plan_006 T=20 也无法拯救伪信号。\\\"Two distinct lead horizons\\\" 升级路径关闭。plan_006 的价值重新定位为：(1) diversity lead 在更长序列中的稳定性，(2) TY power 改善观察，(3) 差分后 permutation Granger 在 T=20 下的表现\\n7. **ECE→perplexity \\\"反向因果\\\"已确认为伪信号 (D017)** — 差分后 ECE→perplexity 0/4 SIG。之前的 post-hoc mechanistic hypothesis 无需讨论。论文只需报告差分前后对比\\n8. **TY 在 N=11 下完全不可用于区分真/伪关联** — d_max sensitivity 分析证实：d_max=0 过度宽松（连 effective_rank 都通过），d_max=1 过度保守（连 token_entropy→distinct_1 都拒绝），adaptive 因 ADF 不稳定而跨 seed 震荡。论文**不能**用 TY 作为\\\"正确答案\\\"。**spurious_rate 必须双版本报告**：含 TY 75%（16 pair 中 12 仅 TY 判伪）、排除 TY 后 62.5%（16 pair 中 10 在 4 种非 TY 方法下一致）。ECE→distinct_2/3 被 TY 判伪但在其余 4 种方法全部 4/4 SIG——这本身是\\\"方法选择改变分类\\\"的例证（Claim 3 素材）\\n9. **ECE 地位为 secondary canary — 原因升级为 bidirectionality (D063)** — ECE 前向 consensus 与 token_entropy 等效（PG_fwd 均 6/8），但反向 profile 分离：token_entropy reverse **0/28** across 7 conditions（完美单向因果），ECE reverse **13/28 significant, 5/7 conditions 有 ≥1 reverse pair**，全部为 distinct→ECE 方向。ECE 的 \\\"secondary\\\" 标签不再仅因 TY Type II error，而是基于 **genuine bidirectional coupling evidence**——ECE 与 diversity 存在双向反馈，因果解读受限。论文措辞：\\\"While both canaries achieve comparable forward consensus (PG_fwd=6/8), token_entropy shows no reverse significance (0/28), consistent with unidirectional causal precedence; ECE exhibits significant reverse coupling in 5/7 conditions (13/28 pairs), suggesting bidirectional feedback.\\\" 这同时服务 Claim 3：单看前向 sig 两者等价，加入反向检验后分离——分析维度选择改变结论\\n10. **d_max sensitivity 曲线是论文的 key figure** — 三组 d_max（0/adaptive/1）× spurious rate 的变化是 Claim 3 的核心可视化，展示\\\"方法选择如何改变结论\\\"\\n11. **统计效力必须显式论证** — Dream 三档一致 CRITICAL：T=10 下 TY 的 DOF 严重不足。论文必须包含 bootstrap/permutation 验证 + plan_006 (T=20) 结果。所有 p-value 必须经 BH FDR 校正\\n12. **\\\"phase diagram\\\" → \\\"regime map\\\"** — 除非 α 分辨率达到 ≥6 个非平凡值 + 提供 α* uncertainty interval，否则用 \\\"predictability regime map\\\" 或 \\\"regime-dependent analysis\\\"。4-5 个 α 值不足以支撑物理学意义上的 \\\"phase diagram\\\"\\n13. **Scheffer et al. 是必引文献** — critical slowing down / early-warning indicators 框架是本研究的知识祖先。论文 Related Work 必须引用并定位\\n14. **CRS 降级为 illustration** — 不列入核心 contribution。CRS 是显然的反应式干预，Dream 三档一致认为 trivial/confounded\\n15. **TY 正式降级为辅助指标 (D015)** — Bootstrap power analysis (N=1000 Monte Carlo): T=10 power=0.05-0.20, T=20 power=0.17, T=30 power=0.29 (d_max≥1)。核心分析框架改为 cross-correlation + permutation test + BH FDR 校正。TY 仅用于 Claim 3 展示（方法敏感性 + power analysis figure），不再作为 lead-lag 判定方法\\n16. **Perplexity 通道确认为伪回归 (D017)** — Permutation Granger 差分版：canary→perplexity 差分后 0/8 SIG (F≈0, p=0.99)，canary→diversity 差分后 12/12 SIG（6 pair × 4 seed 中，token_entropy 和 ECE 各 3 pair 全通过）。差分前后结论翻转（perplexity: 6-8/8→0/8; diversity: 24/24→24/24）是 Claim 3 的关键 figure 素材。**F 值范围**：undifferenced F=74–3370, differenced F=71–7643（全 6 pairs × 4 seeds），范围重叠——之前\\\"F 不降反升\\\"仅对 token_entropy→distinct_1 子集成立，全量数据应以**显著性保持率 24/24→24/24**为核心叙事，不以 F 值大小为论据。论文 Method 节须注明：(1) T=11 first-difference 后 T=10，circular shift 仅 9 个唯一排列，null distribution 高度离散；(2) 尽管如此，diversity 通道 F 值远超任何排列值，结论不受离散性影响\\n17. **f² 效应量报告约束 (D019 更新)** — 差分后 f²=102-435（token_entropy→distinct_1），因 df_resid=5-6 任何真实信号都产生极大 f²（RSS_unrestricted 趋近零）。论文**禁止**用 Cohen 的 0.02/0.15/0.35 基准解读这些 f² 值。正确做法：permutation p-value 为主要推断证据，f² 仅作\\\"效应存在\\\"的定性指示，不做定量比较。**代码层面**：当 df_resid < 20 时，分析脚本输出 `f2_size=\\\"df_too_low\\\"` 而非 small/medium/large 分类（D019 T3 已实现于 permutation_granger.py）\\n18. **2×2 差分对比表是 Claim 3 核心 figure** — channel type（canary→diversity vs canary→perplexity）× differencing status（undifferenced vs differenced）的 2×2 表最紧凑展示\\\"一个方法论选择翻转整条通道的结论\\\"。diversity 通道差分前后均 24/24 SIG（显著性完全保持），F 值范围 undiff 74–3370 / diff 71–7643，部分 pair 略降但显著性完全保持。perplexity 通道差分后归零。比 d_max sensitivity 曲线更直观地展示差分的决定性影响\\n19. **method_ablation 必须双版本报告 (D019)** — spurious_rate 报告含 TY（5-method: 75%）和排除 TY（4-method: 62.5%）两个数字。ECE→distinct_2/3 在 TY 下被判伪但在其余 4 种方法中 4/4 SIG——TY 的 Type II error 驱动的分类差异本身是 Claim 3 素材。论文主文用 4-method 数字作主要结论，5-method 数字用于 Claim 3 方法敏感性讨论\\n20. **ECE canary 可靠性是 regime-dependent (D021)** — α=0.25 下 ECE gen 1 下降 -14%（反向信号），gen 2+ 才上升至 +77%。α=1.0 下 gen 1 直接 +2926%。ECE 作为早期预警 canary 仅在高 α（≥0.50，待 a050 确认）下可靠。低 α 下首代反向信号意味着 ECE 不能用于\\\"一代内预警\\\"。这丰富 Claim 2：canary 可靠性本身是 regime-dependent。D019 \\\"co-primary\\\" 限定为 \\\"high-α regime only\\\"\\n21. **Claim 1 scope 确认为 α≥0.75 (D037, Director 批准)** — α=0.75 Permutation Granger FDR: 3/4 方向显著（ECE→d1 p_fdr=0.046, ECE→d2 p_fdr=0.046, entropy→d2 p_fdr=0.048），entropy→d1 边界 p_fdr=0.054。反向 4/4 全部不显著。Consensus 11/16 robust。**当前 Granger-causal precedence 在 α≥0.75 成立**（α=1.0: 24/24 FDR-sig 4 seeds; α=0.75: 3/4 FDR-sig single seed）。**Single-seed caveat (C001)**: α=0.75 仅 seed=42 验证，待 D026 multi-seed 确认后移除限定。**entropy→d1 borderline (p_fdr=0.054)**: 论文须讨论——3/4 显著 + 1/4 边界，不是 4/4 全过。α≤0.50 均 0/8 FDR-sig，detectability boundary 确认在 0.50→0.75 间\\n22. **α=0.50 null 结果是 positive finding (D025, D037 更新)** — 中等污染下 canary 与 downstream 同步退化（temporal lead 消失），correlation/rule-based 方法（naive, differenced, threshold）100% sig vs 因果检验方法（permG, TY）0% sig 是 Claim 3（method × regime interaction）最极端的例证。论文呈现策略：(1) 此非单调 detectability 模式本身是 regime map 的核心特征；(2) **Detectability boundary 已确认在 0.50-0.75 区间 (D037)**——α=0.75 有 3/4 FDR-sig 信号，practical utility 定位为\\\"高污染场景（α≥0.75）下的早期预警\\\"，而非通用预警工具；(3) α=0.50 null 不是方法失败，是 signal-to-noise 的物理边界\\n23. **α-sweep 全部 single seed 是当前最大弱点 (D026)** — 非单调 dose-response（α=0.25 -40.3% vs α=0.50 -37.3%）和 ECE regime switch 假说均基于 seed=42 的 point estimate，无置信区间。论文中非单调性结论**必须标注 \\\"observed at seed=42; multi-seed confirmation required\\\"**。multi-seed（α=0.25/0.50 各 2-3 seeds）是从 Weak Accept → Accept 的首要条件，优先于 finer α grid（a010/a060）。非单调性在主文仅报告现象（配 drift 表格），\\\"entropy floor\\\" 机制假说放 Discussion\\n24. **α=0.25 双向 Granger 不改变 Claim 1 叙事 (D026)** — d1→entropy raw p=0.023（FDR 后 0.093 不显著），但效应量仅 f²=1.30 vs entropy→d1 的 f²=27.21（21 倍差异）。强单向因果 + 弱反馈，不是对称 coupled dynamics。α=1.0 反方向不显著，α=0.50 反方向 p=0.224。论文可加一句：\\\"marginally significant feedback at mild contamination (f²=1.30, 21× weaker than primary direction)\\\" 但不需要改写核心叙事\\n25. **证据强度排序：Claim 3 > Claim 2 > Claim 1 (D026)** — Reviewer 独立评估。Claim 3（method sensitivity）最强且 novel、well-supported；Claim 2（regime-dependent）次之（但需 multi-seed）；Claim 1（Granger-causal）scope 最窄（仅 α≥0.75）。论文 framing 应确保 Claim 3 的方法论贡献获得足够篇幅，不因 Claim 1 的 \\\"canary prediction\\\" 叙事而被矮化\\n26. **Phase 3 二次衰退仅限 Discussion，不纳入 Core Claims (D035)** — plan_006_s42 (T=18) 发现 gen 13-17 二次 diversity 衰退（d2 -5.8%, d3 -6.9%），两个硬约束：(a) 单 seed，plan_006_s43 确认前不写入论文主体；(b) Phase 3 中 token_entropy 反升 +2.8% 而 diversity 继续下降——canary 在长 horizon 下失效，升级为核心发现会成审稿攻击面。**最佳用法**：Claim 3 补充证据（观察窗口长度改变结论 = 方法敏感性例证）+ Discussion 段落（collapse dynamics richer than saturation, but canary utility diminishes at extended horizons）。不改变 Core Claims / narrative structure / 方法论主线\\n27. **Consensus score 跨 α 比较须标注标准差异 (D038)** — α=0.75 consensus 11/16 robust 使用单种子 method-agreement 标准（≥4/5 方法判定显著），α=1.0 consensus 6/16 robust 使用 4-seed full consensus 标准（≥3/4 seeds × ≥4/5 方法）。两个数字不可直接对比——11/16 > 6/16 不意味着 α=0.75 信号更强，而是标准更宽松。论文中引用时**必须标注标准差异**，禁止直接对比暗示 α=1.0 信号更弱\\n28. **token_entropy→perplexity 在 α=0.75 单种子 consensus 中的 ROBUST 标记不可引用 (D038)** — α=0.75 单种子 consensus 将 token_entropy→perplexity 标为 ROBUST（因多方法一致通过），但 D017 已用 4-seed 差分检验确认该方向为 spurious（自相关驱动，差分后 0/4 sig）。这是单种子 consensus 的已知局限——无法检测共享非平稳趋势导致的伪关联。论文中**不得引用该方向作为 α=0.75 的 robust evidence**，仅限 canary→diversity 方向\\n\", \"total_chars\": 17483, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\":   "toolUseResult": "{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"path\\\": \\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_4/projects/distrib_canary_collapse/paper_framing.md\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"# Paper Framing — distrib_canary_collapse\\\\n\\\\n## Target Venue\\\\n- Primary: **EMNLP 2026 Main Track** (D043, 用户指定)\\\\n- ARR cycle: June 2026 (deadline ~June 15, 待确认具体日期)\\\\n- 格式: ACL 系列模板 (acl.sty), 8 页正文 + unlimited appendix\\\\n- Note: ARR May cycle 已过；June cycle 对齐 EMNLP 2026 commitment deadline\\\\n\\\\n## Narrative Structure (D027: Option B reframe)\\\\n\\\\n**主线**：方法论陷阱 + 多方法共识框架。Canary prediction 为系统性 case study。\\\\n**Claim 呈现顺序**：论文中 Claim 3 领衔 → Claim 2 (regime map) → Claim 1 (case study)。\\\\n**内部编号保持不变**（避免引用混乱），论文呈现时重排。\\\\n\\\\n## Core Claims (3 条，内部编号不变)\\\\n\\\\n1. **Canary→Diversity Granger-Causal Precedence (论文中作为 Case Study Finding)**: token_entropy 对 diversity 退化（distinct_1/2）具有 Granger-causal precedence——领先 1-2 代，差分后仍显著，证据来自多方法一致性：cross-correlation（lag=-1, std=0.0, |r|>0.99, p<0.004, 4/4 seed）、差分交叉相关（显著）、threshold onset（canary gen 1-2, diversity gen 3-4）、**permutation Granger（差分后仍 4/4 seed 显著，F=612-2615）**。论文可用措辞 (D037 更新)：*\\\\\\\"We demonstrate Granger-causal precedence of canary metrics at α≥0.75: α=1.0 achieves 24/24 FDR-sig (4 seeds), α=0.75 achieves 3/4 FDR-sig with the fourth borderline (p_fdr=0.054; single seed, pending multi-seed confirmation C001). Consistent temporal precedence (cross-correlation lag=+1, r=0.80–0.94) is observed across all contamination levels, though formal Granger tests at α≤0.50 do not reach FDR-corrected significance at T=11 (power analysis: §X).\\\\\\\"*升级上限：Granger ≠ true causality（Granger 1969），Discussion 一句话声明；双变量模型无法排除未观测混淆因子（详见 Reviewer Concerns 表）。ECE 前向 consensus 与 token_entropy 等效但存在 bidirectional coupling（详见约束 #9）。注意：TY Granger 在 N=11 下不可用于区分真/伪关联（见 Claim 3），核心证据链基于多方法一致性而非单一 TY 背书。**D063 泛化证据**：Table 7 跨 7 conditions（3 architectures × 2 scales × 2 domains）K≥4=5/8-6/8。**Diversity-only subset（排除→ppl pairs）consensus 41/42 = 97.6%**，仅 1.4B s42 的 1 个 diversity pair 未达 K≥4。→ppl pairs 0/14 达 K≥4（结构性失败，非方法缺陷），canary→perplexity 的系统性弱信号作为 explicit finding 报告——diversity collapse 是可预测的下游维度，perplexity 不是。\\\\n2. **Regime-Dependent Predictability**: 可预测性取决于 collapse 维度和 contamination regime——canary→diversity 在差分后仍 robust（6/6 pair × 4/4 seed），但 canary→perplexity **经差分检验确认为伪回归**（0/4 seed, F≈0, p=0.99），未差分时的\\\\\\\"信号\\\\\\\"（F=16-36）完全由共享非平稳趋势驱动（D017）。ECE→perplexity 的反向因果同理存疑。**Claim 2 reframing (D022)**: 从\\\\\\\"不同 α 下 lead time 不同\\\\\\\"升级为\\\\\\\"不同 α 下 **detectability** 不同\\\\\\\"——α=0.25 差分 Granger 0/8 FDR sig vs α=1.0 的 24/24，同一 pair 的统计可检测性随 contamination level 剧变。**D037 更新**：5-point regime map 完整，detectability 在 α=0.50→0.75 间出现阶跃（0/8→3/8 FDR-sig），α=0.75 consensus 11/16 robust。**Evidence strategy (Reviewer 共识)**：Primary = raw drift magnitude dose-response（distinct_1: -14%@α=0 → -40%@α=0.25 → ~-93%@α=1.0），方法无关、不可争议；Secondary = 差分 Granger FDR sig rate vs α，展示 detectability boundary at α~0.75。**不用 naive_xcorr 做 primary**——与 Claim 3 自相矛盾。Predictability regime map 划定可预测/不可预测边界（\\\\\\\"phase diagram\\\\\\\" 需 ≥6 个 α 值 + uncertainty interval 才成立，当前 5 个 α 值下用 \\\\\\\"regime map\\\\\\\"）。\\\\n3. **Method Selection Sensitivity and Regime-Dependent Validity**（升级自 Methodological Rigor；D022 升级候选：method × regime interaction）: 在典型 generation 数（N≈10）下，lead-lag 结论对分析方法和超参数高度敏感，且**同一方法在不同 α regime 下结论翻转**（diff Granger: α=1.0 24/24 FDR sig → α=0.25 0/8）。升级措辞候选：\\\\\\\"Method sensitivity is compounded by regime dependence: the same statistical test applied to the same metric pair can yield significant or null results depending on contamination level\\\\\\\"。**三层证据结构**：**(Layer 1)** naive Pearson 找到 16/16 显著——方法宽松时一切 \\\\\\\"显著\\\\\\\"；**(Layer 2)** 一阶差分翻转整条 perplexity 通道（8/8→0/8 SIG）而 diversity 通道不变（12/12→12/12）——一个方法论选择决定通道存亡（D017）；**(Layer 3)** TY d_max sensitivity 使结论在 ~0%↔94% spurious rate 间震荡——超参数选择同样致命。方法论贡献不是\\\\\\\"提供正确的检验\\\\\\\"，而是**暴露方法选择敏感性并提出多方法一致性框架**——只有跨方法（cross-corr + 差分 + threshold + permutation Granger）和跨 seed 都一致的关联才可信。这是迭代训练文献中被忽视的方法论陷阱。\\\\n\\\\n## Story Spine (D027: Option B reframe)\\\\n\\\\n- **Problem**: Model collapse 文献中的 lead-lag 分析（\\\\\\\"哪些指标预测崩溃\\\\\\\"）日益增多（Du et al., SIGMA, DCES），但**方法选择对结论的影响未被系统性审视**。Du et al. 非正式观察到 ECE 领先 accuracy，仅限单指标对、1-3 代、无统计检验。现有工作的方法论基础薄弱：无差分检验（不排除伪回归）、无多方法交叉验证、无多重比较校正、无 regime-dependent 分析。\\\\n\\\\n- **Gap**: 没有工作系统性回答：(1) lead-lag 结论在多大程度上取决于分析方法的选择？(2) 同一统计检验在不同 contamination regime 下结论是否稳定？(3) 如何建立可靠的 lead-lag 判定框架？**Scheffer et al. (2009, 2012)** 在生态/气候科学中建立了临界转变早期预警框架，但尚未被 model collapse 文献引入，其方法论严谨性（多方法交叉验证 + regime-dependent analysis）恰好是本领域所缺失的。\\\\n\\\\n- **Our approach**: 以 canary prediction for model collapse 为系统性 case study，暴露 lead-lag 分析中的三个方法论陷阱：(1) 方法选择敏感性——同一数据集上 5 种统计方法从 100% 显著到 0% 显著（Claim 3）；(2) 未差分的伪回归——canary→perplexity 通道在差分后完全消失（Claim 1 case study）；(3) regime-dependent validity——同一检验在不同 α 下结论翻转（Claim 2）。在此基础上提出**多方法一致性共识框架**（multi-method consensus framework）作为解决方案：只有跨方法（cross-corr + 差分 + threshold + permutation Granger）和跨 seed 都一致的关联才可信。\\\\n\\\\n- **Headline result (D037 更新)**: \\\\\\\"In iterative synthetic training with Pythia-410M, naive correlation identifies 100% of canary-collapse metric pairs as significant, while differenced permutation Granger retains only 25% — and this method-dependent gap is compounded by regime dependence: the same test applied to the same metric pair yields 24/24 FDR-significant results at α=1.0, 3/4 at α=0.75, but 0/8 at α≤0.50. We propose a multi-method consensus framework and, applying it, identify token entropy as a robust 1-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse at α≥0.75.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n- **Title direction**: \\\\\\\"Method Matters: Pitfalls and a Multi-Method Consensus Framework for Lead-Lag Analysis in Iterative Training Collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n- **文献定位 (保留)**：SIGMA 提供谱监控但无 lead-time 量化；DCES 反应式而非预测式；Du et al. 是最接近的经验前辈但方法论不足；Kalinowski 拓扑 CI 针对表征坍缩非合成数据退化且计算昂贵。Truong & Truong 证明一阶相变下标准早期预警指标失效。理论分析方面：Seddik et al. (2024), Gerstgrasser et al. (2024), Dey et al. (2024) 提供互补理论视角但不涉及方法论敏感性。**我们与 Du et al. 的核心区别**：(1) 多指标家族系统对比；(2) 多方法交叉验证 + d_max sensitivity；(3) regime-dependent mapping；(4) **方法选择敏感性本身是主要贡献**（不再是\\\\\\\"独立贡献\\\\\\\"而是\\\\\\\"核心贡献\\\\\\\"）。\\\\n\\\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns & Responses\\\\n\\\\n| Concern | Response Strategy | Required Experiment |\\\\n|---------|-------------------|---------------------|\\\\n| \\\\\\\"Canary 不预测 perplexity，实用价值有限\\\\\\\" | 差分检验已确认 canary→perplexity 为伪回归（D017）。Framing 转为\\\\\\\"canary 预测 diversity collapse\\\\\\\"——diversity 是 collapse 最早可观测维度，实用价值在于比 perplexity 更早预警。perplexity 通道的伪信号本身是方法论贡献（Claim 3 例证） | 已有数据（D017 确认） |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"ECE 和 token_entropy 对 perplexity 方向相反？\\\\\\\" | 差分后两者对 方法（Du et al.）；(b) pitfall 不止于 naive vs formal——differencing × regime 交互是非 trivial 的（差分翻转整条 perplexity 通道但保留 diversity 通道）；(c) d_max sensitivity 曲线证明即使选了 TY，超参数仍致命 | 已有 method_ablation + α-sweep 数据 |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"与 critical slowing down 文献的关系？\\\\\\\" | §Related Work 引用 Scheffer et al. 2009/2012 并定位为\\\\\\\"将 tipping-point early-warning 框架引入 model collapse\\\\\\\"。讨论 rising autocorrelation/variance 类比 | 文献工作 |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"双变量 Granger 无法排除混淆因子\\\\\\\" | token_entropy 和 ECE 可能共享信息源（均捕捉 output distribution shape），双变量 Granger 无法排除未观测混淆因子。论文 Discussion 须显式声明 Granger precedence ≠ true causality（Granger 1969），并讨论多变量 VAR 或条件 Granger 作为 future work。多方法一致性（4 种独立方法收敛）部分缓解但不消除此顾虑 | Discussion 声明 + future work |\\\\n\\\\n## Venue Fit\\\\n\\\\nEMNLP 2026 Main Track 优势：\\\\n- NLP 社区对 model collapse / data contamination 高度关注，audience 精准\\\\n- ACL/EMNLP 接受 empirical methodology + analysis paper，不要求 SOTA\\\\n- 方法论 pitfalls + consensus framework 对 NLP 从业者直接可操作\\\\n- Iterative training / synthetic data 是 LLM 时代热点话题\\\\n\\\\n风险：\\\\n- 如果被认为\\\\\\\"只是 empirical study 没有算法贡献\\\\\\\"→ 多方法一致性框架 + 方法选择敏感性分析本身是方法论贡献（Claim 3）；CRS 降级为 illustration 不作独立贡献\\\\n- 模型规模偏小（Pythia-410M + GPT-2-medium）→ 诚实标注 limitation + scaling trend\\\\n- 时间紧凑（~1 个月写作窗口）→ Paper Agent Full Draft 模式已激活，框架和数据就绪\\\\n\\\\n## Key Narrative Constraints (from MVP + plan_003)\\\\n\\\\n1. **不能 claim \\\\\\\"canary predicts model collapse\\\\\\\"** — 只能 claim \\\\\\\"canary predicts diversity collapse\\\\\\\"。token_entropy→perplexity 经差分检验确认为伪回归（0/4 seed, F≈0, p=0.99, D017），未差分时的\\\\\\\"弱信号\\\\\\\"完全由共享非平稳趋势驱动。论文措辞用\\\\\\\"spurious association confirmed by first-differencing\\\\\\\"，不再用\\\\\\\"弱信号\\\\\\\"\\\\n2. **ECE→perplexity 和 token_entropy→perplexity 均为伪信号 (D017)** — 差分后两者均 0/4 seed 显著。未差分时观察到的\\\\\\\"方向相反\\\\\\\"（token_entropy 领先 vs perplexity 领先 ECE）是共享趋势的不同表现形式。论文报告差分前后对比作为方法论教训，不再需要机制性解释\\\\n3. **effective_rank 和 self_bleu 作为 canary 失败** — 必须全量报告，不能只报正面结果\\\\n4. **distinct_3 必须放 supplementary，不能完全省略** — max_lag=7 下升为 SIGNIFICANT 但 lag_std>1.5（双峰：seed 42 总是 -5，其余 -1/-2），维持 D008 降级策略，主文聚焦 distinct_1/2。Supplementary 需给完整结果 + 解释 lag instability（高阶 n-gram diversity 对短序列随机变异更敏感）。distinct_3 的不稳定性本身支持 \\\\\\\"predictability boundary\\\\\\\" 主题——即使 diversity 维度内部也有可预测性梯度\\\\n5. **max_lag=7 确认核心 lead 无截断** — token_entropy→distinct_1 lag=-1（std=0.0）、ECE→distinct_2 lag=-1（std=0.0）在 max_lag=5/7 下完全一致。token_entropy→perplexity lag=-5 虽非截断伪影，但已确认为非平稳伪回归（D017）\\\\n6. **~~Dual-timescale 升级条件~~ — 已失效 (D017)** — perplexity 通道经差分确认为伪回归，即使 plan_006 T=20 也无法拯救伪信号。\\\\\\\"Two distinct lead horizons\\\\\\\" 升级路径关闭。plan_006 的价值重新定位为：(1) diversity lead 在更长序列中的稳定性，(2) TY power 改善观察，(3) 差分后 permutation Granger 在 T=20 下的表现\\\\n7. **ECE→perplexity \\\\\\\"反向因果\\\\\\\"已确认为伪信号 (D017)** — 差分后 ECE→perplexity 0/4 SIG。之前的 post-hoc mechanistic hypothesis 无需讨论。论文只需报告差分前后对比\\\\n8. **TY 在 N=11 下完全不可用于区分真/伪关联** — d_max sensitivity 分析证实：d_max=0 过度宽松（连 effective_rank 都通过），d_max=1 过度保守（连 token_entropy→distinct_1 都拒绝），adaptive 因 ADF 不稳定而跨 seed 震荡。论文**不能**用 TY 作为\\\\\\\"正确答案\\\\\\\"。**spurious_rate 必须双版本报告**：含 TY 75%（16 pair 中 12 仅 TY 判伪）、排除 TY 后 62.5%（16 pair 中 10 在 4 种非 TY 方法下一致）。ECE→distinct_2/3 被 TY 判伪但在其余 4 种方法全部 4/4 SIG——这本身是\\\\\\\"方法选择改变分类\\\\\\\"的例证（Claim 3 素材）\\\\n9. **ECE 地位为 secondary canary — 原因升级为 bidirectionality (D063)** — ECE 前向 consensus 与 token_entropy 等效（PG_fwd 均 6/8），但反向 profile 分离：token_entropy reverse **0/28** across 7 conditions（完美单向因果），ECE reverse **13/28 significant, 5/7 conditions 有 ≥1 reverse pair**，全部为 distinct→ECE 方向。ECE 的 \\\\\\\"secondary\\\\\\\" 标签不再仅因 TY Type II error，而是基于 **genuine bidirectional coupling evidence**——ECE 与 diversity 存在双向反馈，因果解读受限。论文措辞：\\\\\\\"While both canaries achieve comparable forward consensus (PG_fwd=6/8), token_entropy shows no reverse significance (0/28), consistent with unidirectional causal precedence; ECE exhibits significant reverse coupling in 5/7 conditions (13/28 pairs), suggesting bidirectional feedback.\\\\\\\" 这同时服务 Claim 3：单看前向 sig 两者等价，加入反向检验后分离——分析维度选择改变结论\\\\n10. **d_max sensitivity 曲线是论文的 key figure** — 三组 d_max（0/adaptive/1）× spurious rate 的变化是 Claim 3 的核心可视化，展示\\\\\\\"方法选择如何改变结论\\\\\\\"\\\\n11. **统计效力必须显式论证** — Dream 三档一致 CRITICAL：T=10 下 TY 的 DOF 严重不足。论文必须包含 bootstrap/permutation 验证 + plan_006 (T=20) 结果。所有 p-value 必须经 BH FDR 校正\\\\n12. **\\\\\\\"phase diagram\\\\\\\" → \\\\\\\"regime map\\\\\\\"** — 除非 α 分辨率达到 ≥6 个非平凡值 + 提供 α* uncertainty interval，否则用 \\\\\\\"predictability regime map\\\\\\\" 或 \\\\\\\"regime-dependent analysis\\\\\\\"。4-5 个 α 值不足以支撑物理学意义上的 \\\\\\\"phase diagram\\\\\\\"\\\\n13. **Scheffer et al. 是必引文献** — critical slowing down / early-warning indicators 框架是本研究的知识祖先。论文 Related Work 必须引用并定位\\\\n14. **CRS 降级为 illustration** — 不列入核心 contribution。CRS 是显然的反应式干预，Dream 三档一致认为 trivial/confounded\\\\n15. **TY 正式降级为辅助指标 (D015)** — Bootstrap power analysis (N=1000 Monte Carlo): T=10 power=0.05-0.20, T=20 power=0.17, T=30 power=0.29 (d_max≥1)。核心分析框架改为 cross-correlation + permutation test + BH FDR 校正。TY 仅用于 Claim 3 展示（方法敏感性 + power analysis figure），不再作为 lead-lag 判定方法\\\\n16. **Perplexity 通道确认为伪回归 (D017)** — Permutation Granger 差分版：canary→perplexity 差分后 0/8 SIG (F≈0, p=0.99)，canary→diversity 差分后 12/12 SIG（6 pair × 4 seed 中，token_entropy 和 ECE 各 3 pair 全通过）。差分前后结论翻转（perplexity: 6-8/8→0/8; diversity: 24/24→24/24）是 Claim 3 的关键 figure 素材。**F 值范围**：undifferenced F=74–3370, differenced F=71–7643（全 6 pairs × 4 seeds），范围重叠——之前\\\\\\\"F 不降反升\\\\\\\"仅对 token_entropy→distinct_1 子集成立，全量数据应以**显著性保持率 24/24→24/24**为核心叙事，不以 F 值大小为论据。论文 Method 节须注明：(1) T=11 first-difference 后 T=10，circular shift 仅 9 个唯一排列，null distribution 高度离散；(2) 尽管如此，diversity 通道 F Consistent temporal precedence (cross-correlation lag=+1, r=0.80–0.94) is observed across all contamination levels, though formal Granger tests at α≤0.50 do not reach FDR-corrected significance at T=11 (power analysis: §X).\\\"*升级上限：Granger ≠ true causality（Granger 1969），Discussion 一句话声明；双变量模型无法排除未观测混淆因子（详见 Reviewer Concerns 表）。ECE 前向 consensus 与 token_entropy 等效但存在 bidirectional coupling（详见约束 #9）。注意：TY Granger 在 N=11 下不可用于区分真/伪关联（见 Claim 3），核心证据链基于多方法一致性而非单一 TY 背书。**D063 泛化证据**：Table 7 跨 7 conditions（3 architectures × 2 scales × 2 domains）K≥4=5/8-6/8。**Diversity-only subset（排除→ppl pairs）consensus 41/42 = 97.6%**，仅 1.4B s42 的 1 个 diversity pair 未达 K≥4。→ppl pairs 0/14 达 K≥4（结构性失败，非方法缺陷），canary→perplexity 的系统性弱信号作为 explicit finding 报告——diversity collapse 是可预测的下游维度，perplexity 不是。\\n2. **Regime-Dependent Predictability**: 可预测性取决于 collapse 维度和 contamination regime——canary→diversity 在差分后仍 robust（6/6 pair × 4/4 seed），但 canary→perplexity **经差分检验确认为伪回归**（0/4 seed, F≈0, p=0.99），未差分时的\\\"信号\\\"（F=16-36）完全由共享非平稳趋势驱动（D017）。ECE→perplexity 的反向因果同理存疑。**Claim 2 reframing (D022)**: 从\\\"不同 α 下 lead time 不同\\\"升级为\\\"不同 α 下 **detectability** 不同\\\"——α=0.25 差分 Granger 0/8 FDR sig vs α=1.0 的 24/24，同一 pair 的统计可检测性随 contamination level 剧变。**D037 更新**：5-point regime map 完整，detectability 在 α=0.50→0.75 间出现阶跃（0/8→3/8 FDR-sig），α=0.75 consensus 11/16 robust。**Evidence strategy (Reviewer 共识)**：Primary = raw drift magnitude dose-response（distinct_1: -14%@α=0 → -40%@α=0.25 → ~-93%@α=1.0），方法无关、不可争议；Secondary = 差分 Granger FDR sig rate vs α，展示 detectability boundary at α~0.75。**不用 naive_xcorr 做 primary**——与 Claim 3 自相矛盾。Predictability regime map 划定可预测/不可预测边界（\\\"phase diagram\\\" 需 ≥6 个 α 值 + uncertainty interval 才成立，当前 5 个 α 值下用 \\\"regime map\\\"）。\\n3. **Method Selection Sensitivity and Regime-Dependent Validity**（升级自 Methodological Rigor；D022 升级候选：method × regime interaction）: 在典型 generation 数（N≈10）下，lead-lag 结论对分析方法和超参数高度敏感，且**同一方法在不同 α regime 下结论翻转**（diff Granger: α=1.0 24/24 FDR sig → α=0.25 0/8）。升级措辞候选：\\\"Method sensitivity is compounded by regime dependence: the same statistical test applied to the same metric pair can yield significant or null results depending on contamination level\\\"。**三层证据结构**：**(Layer 1)** naive Pearson 找到 16/16 显著——方法宽松时一切 \\\"显著\\\"；**(Layer 2)** 一阶差分翻转整条 perplexity 通道（8/8→0/8 SIG）而 diversity 通道不变（12/12→12/12）——一个方法论选择决定通道存亡（D017）；**(Layer 3)** TY d_max sensitivity 使结论在 ~0%↔94% spurious rate 间震荡——超参数选择同样致命。方法论贡献不是\\\"提供正确的检验\\\"，而是**暴露方法选择敏感性并提出多方法一致性框架**——只有跨方法（cross-corr + 差分 + threshold + permutation Granger）和跨 seed 都一致的关联才可信。这是迭代训练文献中被忽视的方法论陷阱。\\n\\n## Story Spine (D027: Option B reframe)\\n\\n- **Problem**: Model collapse 文献中的 lead-lag 分析（\\\"哪些指标预测崩溃\\\"）日益增多（Du et al., SIGMA, DCES），但**方法选择对结论的影响未被系统性审视**。Du et al. 非正式观察到 ECE 领先 accuracy，仅限单指标对、1-3 代、无统计检验。现有工作的方法论基础薄弱：无差分检验（不排除伪回归）、无多方法交叉验证、无多重比较校正、无 regime-dependent 分析。\\n\\n- **Gap**: 没有工作系统性回答：(1) lead-lag 结论在多大程度上取决于分析方法的选择？(2) 同一统计检验在不同 contamination regime 下结论是否稳定？(3) 如何建立可靠的 lead-lag 判定框架？**Scheffer et al. (2009, 2012)** 在生态/气候科学中建立了临界转变早期预警框架，但尚未被 model collapse 文献引入，其方法论严谨性（多方法交叉验证 + regime-dependent analysis）恰好是本领域所缺失的。\\n\\n- **Our approach**: 以 canary prediction for model collapse 为系统性 case study，暴露 lead-lag 分析中的三个方法论陷阱：(1) 方法选择敏感性——同一数据集上 5 种统计方法从 100% 显著到 0% 显著（Claim 3）；(2) 未差分的伪回归——canary→perplexity 通道在差分后完全消失（Claim 1 case study）；(3) regime-dependent validity——同一检验在不同 α 下结论翻转（Claim 2）。在此基础上提出**多方法一致性共识框架**（multi-method consensus framework）作为解决方案：只有跨方法（cross-corr + 差分 + threshold + permutation Granger）和跨 seed 都一致的关联才可信。\\n\\n- **Headline result (D037 更新)**: \\\"In iterative synthetic training with Pythia-410M, naive correlation identifies 100% of canary-collapse metric pairs as significant, while differenced permutation Granger retains only 25% — and this method-dependent gap is compounded by regime dependence: the same test applied to the same metric pair yields 24/24 FDR-significant results at α=1.0, 3/4 at α=0.75, but 0/8 at α≤0.50. We propose a multi-method consensus framework and, applying it, identify token entropy as a robust 1-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse at α≥0.75.\\\"\\n\\n- **Title direction**: \\\"Method Matters: Pitfalls and a Multi-Method Consensus Framework for Lead-Lag Analysis in Iterative Training Collapse\\\"\\n\\n- **文献定位 (保留)**：SIGMA 提供谱监控但无 lead-time 量化；DCES 反应式而非预测式；Du et al. 是最接近的经验前辈但方法论不足；Kalinowski 拓扑 CI 针对表征坍缩非合成数据退化且计算昂贵。Truong & Truong 证明一阶相变下标准早期预警指标失效。理论分析方面：Seddik et al. (2024), Gerstgrasser et al. (2024), Dey et al. (2024) 提供互补理论视角但不涉及方法论敏感性。**我们与 Du et al. 的核心区别**：(1) 多指标家族系统对比；(2) 多方法交叉验证 + d_max sensitivity；(3) regime-dependent mapping；(4) **方法选择敏感性本身是主要贡献**（不再是\\\"独立贡献\\\"而是\\\"核心贡献\\\"）。\\n\\n## Anticipated diversity 对短序列随机变异更敏感）。distinct_3 的不稳定性本身支持 \\\"predictability boundary\\\" 主题——即使 diversity 维度内部也有可预测性梯度\\n5. **max_lag=7 确认核心 lead 无截断** — token_entropy→distinct_1 lag=-1（std=0.0）、ECE→distinct_2 ```text
This is a very comprehensive paper framing document. Let me review it against the current experimental evidence:

**Key observations:**

1. **Core Claims structure is well-defined:**
   - Claim 1: Canary→Diversity Granger-Causal Precedence (case study)
   - Claim 2: Regime-Dependent Predictability (dose-response map)
   - Claim 3: Method Selection Sensitivity (methodological contribution - LEAD claim)

2. **Evidence gaps I can identify:**

   a. **Table 7 cross-architecture validation (D063)**: Claims "7 conditions (3 architectures × 2 scales × 2 domains)". 

Looking at the experimental status across architectures and configurations, I have solid results for Pythia-410M and GPT-2m with fp32, though one GPT-2m seed shows a precision downgrade with bf16. The remaining architectures—Pythia-1.4B, Pythia-6.9B, Qwen3.5-0.8B, and SmolLM3-3B—are still in progress across various precision settings and generation parameters.

The bf16 configurations are problematic because they're triggering downgrades in the D079 metric, which undermines the "7 conditions" framing. More critically, I'm missing the α=0.60 fp32 point due to a linear algebra error, and this gap is essential for identifying where the permG transition occurs. For the multi-seed validation that the paper identifies as the key criterion for upgrading from Weak Accept to Accept, I need to check my current seed coverage across different α values—I have solid coverage at α=0.00 and α=0.50, but α=0.10 and α=0.25 are undersampled, and I haven't finished tallying the higher α values yet.

The intervention validation experiments are all working, though the paper only frames them as illustrative rather than core evidence. There's also a mismatch between what the ROADMAP claims about claim ordering and what the Phase 3 report shows for the permG numbers in the dose-response map—the ROADMAP references 9/24 but Phase 3 shows 0, which suggests I need to reconcile these discrepancies.

The LinAlgError in metrics.py is now the main blocker preventing fp32 experiments on Pythia-410M from running, though it doesn't seem to affect the larger models. With the deadline around June 15, I have about 28 days to complete everything, which should be manageable if I can resolve this error quickly since it's holding up three experiments. I've already flagged the critical issues, so now I should focus on mapping out what experiments are still needed to support each paper claim.

I should focus my report on two things: the overall coverage assessment with a claim-by-claim breakdown, and the impact of the LinAlgError blocking critical experiments.

79	## 分析
80	
81	> **Baseline 说明**：gen0 指标是各数据集的 baseline 参考值，所有 drift 均相对 gen0 计算。C4 原始数据集 4,500 条，WikiText 原始 716,506 条，但 `mix_datasets()` 在 α=0.50 时将两者均衡为 C4 ~9,000 条 (4,500+4,500) vs WikiText ~10,000 条 (5,000+5,000)，训练量差异仅 ~10%。
82	>
83	> **bf16 Confound（D078）**：C4 实验 (alpha_sweep_c4_a050_s42_bf16) 使用 bf16 训练，其他 α sweep 实验（如 alpha_sweep_a050_s42）使用 fp32。因此 **ppl 绝对值不可直接跨数据集比较**。Drift 百分比和指标排序不受 bf16 影响。
84	
85	### 1. 根因：领域特征差异（非训练量差异）
86	
87	`mix_datasets()` 将 C4 均衡为 ~9,000 条、WikiText 为 ~10,000 条（差异 ~10%），每代 epochs 分别为 ~2.2 和 ~2.0。训练量几乎相同，因此 collapse 轨迹差异的主因**不是过拟合程度**，而是以下候选因素：
88	1. **文本长度差异**: C4 ~2,499 chars vs WikiText ~768 chars，更长的序列可能导致不同的优化动态和模式坍缩路径
89	2. **bf16 vs fp32**: C4 使用 bf16 训练，精度损失在多代迭代中累积（D078）
90	3. **领域分布差异**: web crawl (C4) vs 百科 (WikiText) 的 token 分布特征不同
91	4. **基模型校准差异**: Pythia-410M 对两个领域的初始适配度不同（gen0 ppl: C4=24.4 vs Wiki=26.9）
92	
93	### 2. 两种不同的 collapse 模式
94	
95	- **C4: confidence collapse**。entropy 从 gen0 到 gen10 单调下降（3.14→1.94, -38.2%），泛化能力逐代退化（ppl drift +402%，ECE 从 0.011→0.258）。词汇多样性保持相对较好（distinct_2 drift -24.6%）。C4 的长文本序列（~2,499 chars）可能使模型更容易在迭代中陷入特定模式。
96	
97	- **WikiText: diversity collapse**。ppl drift 温和（+21.1%），但迭代合成数据逐代削减词汇多样性（distinct_2 drift -51.5%，distinct_3 drift -52.4%），模型偏好高频模式而放弃低频表达。WikiText 的短文本（~768 chars）可能使 n-gram 分布更容易被合成数据扭曲。
98	
99	### 3. MAUVE baseline 差异
100	
101	WikiText gen0 MAUVE 为 0.034（C4 gen0 为 0.691）。这是两个数据集的 baseline 特征差异：WikiText 包含大量短文本、空行、标题行，合成文本分布与原始分布的匹配天然困难；C4 经过清洗，文本质量更均匀，baseline MAUVE 更高。两者的 MAUVE drift 方向一致（均下降），但起点不同。
102	
103	### 4. bf16 vs fp32 混淆因素
104	
105	C4 实验使用 bf16，WikiText 使用 fp32。**ppl 绝对值不可直接跨实验比较**（D078 决策）。bf16 精度损失可能在多代迭代中累积，但 entropy drift -38.2% 的量级无法仅由精度差异解释，因此 bf16 不改变 collapse 模式的定性结论。Drift 百分比和指标排序不受影响。
106	
107	### 结论
108	
109	C4 与 WikiText 在 α=0.50 下展现不同 collapse 模式。`mix_datasets()` 将两者训练量均衡至 ~9k vs ~10k（~2.2 vs ~2.0 epochs/gen），**训练量差异仅 10%，不足以解释 collapse 轨迹的巨大分歧**。C4 呈 confidence collapse（entropy -38.2%, ppl +402%），WikiText 呈 diversity collapse（d2 -51.5%, ppl +21.1%）。候选根因包括文本长度差异（C4 3.3× longer）、bf16 vs fp32 精度效应、领域分布差异、以及基模型对两域的初始校准差异。ppl 绝对值受 bf16 confound 影响（D078），不可直接跨实验比较。
110

0.034 |\n| 5 | 0.100 | 0.026 |\n| 10 | 0.078 | 0.021 |\n| **Δ%** | **-88.7** | **-38.4** |\n\n### ECE (Expected Calibration Error)\n\n| Gen | C4 | WikiText |\n|-----|-------|----------|\n| 0 | 0.011 | 0.011 |\n| 5 | 0.171 | 0.098 |\n| 10 | 0.258 | 0.102 |\n| **Δ%** | **+23.5x** | **+9.4x** |\n\n## 分析\n\n> **Baseline 说明**：gen0 指标是各数据集的 baseline 参考值，所有 drift 均相对 gen0 计算。C4 原始数据集 4,500 条，WikiText 原始 716,506 条，但 `mix_datasets()` 在 α=0.50 时将两者均衡为 C4 ~9,000 条 (4,500+4,500) vs WikiText ~10,000 条 (5,000+5,000)，训练量差异仅 ~10%。\n>\n> **bf16 Confound（D078）**：C4 实验 (alpha_sweep_c4_a050_s42_bf16) 使用 bf16 训练，其他 α sweep 实验（如 alpha_sweep_a050_s42）使用 fp32。因此 **ppl 绝对值不可直接跨数据集比较**。Drift 百分比和指标排序不受 bf16 影响。\n\n### 1. 根因：领域特征差异（非训练量差异）\n\n`mix_datasets()` 将 C4 均衡为 ~9,000 条、WikiText 为 ~10,000 条（差异 ~10%），每代 epochs 分别为 ~2.2 和 ~2.0。训练量几乎相同，因此 collapse 轨迹差异的主因**不是过拟合程度**，而是以下候选因素：\n1. **文本长度差异**: C4 ~2,499 chars vs WikiText ~768 chars，更长的序列可能导致不同的优化动态和模式坍缩路径\n2. **bf16 vs fp32**: C4 使用 bf16 训练，精度损失在多代迭代中累积（D078）\n3. **领域分布差异**: web crawl (C4) vs 百科 (WikiText) 的 token 分布特征不同\n4. **基模型校准差异**: Pythia-410M 对两个领域的初始适配度不同（gen0 ppl: C4=24.4 vs Wiki=26.9）\n\n### 2. 两种不同的 collapse 模式\n\n- **C4: confidence collapse**。entropy 从 gen0 到 gen10 单调下降（3.14→1.94, -38.2%），泛化能力逐代退化（ppl drift +402%，ECE 从 0.011→0.258）。词汇多样性保持相对较好（distinct_2 drift -24.6%）。C4 的长文本序列（~2,499 chars）可能使模型更容易在迭代中陷入特定模式。\n\n- **WikiText: diversity collapse**。ppl drift 温和（+21.1%），但迭代合成数据逐代削减词汇多样性（distinct_2 drift -51.5%，distinct_3 drift -52.4%），模型偏好高频模式而放弃低频表达。WikiText 的短文本（~768 chars）可能使 n-gram 分布更容易被合成数据扭曲。\n\n### 3. MAUVE baseline 差异\n\nWikiText gen0 MAUVE 为 0.034（C4 gen0 为 0.691）。这是两个数据集的 baseline 特征差异：WikiText 包含大量短文本、空行、标题行，合成文本分布与原始分布的匹配天然困难；C4 经过清洗，文本质量更均匀，baseline MAUVE 更高。两者的 MAUVE drift 方向一致（均下降），但起点不同。\n\n### 4. bf16 vs fp32 混淆因素\n\nC4 实验使用 bf16，WikiText 使用 fp32。**ppl 绝对值不可直接跨实验比较**（D078 决策）。bf16 精度损失可能在多代迭代中累积，但 entropy drift -38.2% 的量级无法仅由精度差异解释，因此 bf16 不改变 collapse 模式的定性结论。Drift 百分比和指标排序不受影响。\n\n### 结论\n\nC4 与 WikiText 在 α=0.50 下展现不同 collapse 模式。`mix_datasets()` 将两者训练量均衡至 ~9k vs ~10k（~2.2 vs ~2.0 epochs/gen），**训练量差异仅 10%，不足以解释 collapse 轨迹的巨大分歧**。C4 呈 confidence collapse（entropy -38.2%, ppl +402%），WikiText 呈 diversity collapse（d2 -51.5%, ppl +21.1%）。候选根因包括文本长度差异（C4 3.3× longer）、bf16 vs fp32 精度效应、领域分布差异、以及基模型对两域的初始校准差异。ppl 绝对值受 bf16 confound 影响（D078），不可直接跨实验比较。\n",

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Canary and downstream metrics for model collapse detection.\\n\\nComputes per-generation metrics on held-out data and generated samples.\\nAll compute_* functions share the signature:\\n    (model, tokenizer, eval_dataset, gen_dataset=None, **kwargs) -> float | dict\\n\\nDependencies: torch, transformers, mauve-text, nltk\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport math\\nfrom collections import Counter\\nfrom itertools import combinations\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom nltk.translate.bleu_score import SmoothingFunction, sentence_bleu\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Text-surface metrics\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef compute_token_entropy(model, tokenizer, eval_dataset, gen_dataset=None,\\n                          max_samples=512, max_length=256, batch_size=8):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Mean next-token entropy of `model` on `eval_dataset` (held-out real text).\\n\\n    Lower entropy over generations signals the model is becoming overly\\n    confident / less diverse — a collapse canary.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    model.eval()\\n    device = next(model.parameters()).device\\n    total_entropy = 0.0\\n    total_tokens = 0\\n\\n    texts = eval_dataset[\\\"text\\\"][:max_samples]\\n    for i in range(0, len(texts), batch_size):\\n        batch_texts = texts[i:i + batch_size]\\n        enc = tokenizer(batch_texts, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\", truncation=True,\\n                        max_length=max_length, padding=True)\\n        input_ids = enc[\\\"input_ids\\\"].to(device)\\n        attention_mask = enc[\\\"attention_mask\\\"].to(device)\\n\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            logits = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask).logits\\n\\n        # shift: predict token t+1 from position t\\n        shift_logits = logits[:, :-1, :]\\n        shift_mask = attention_mask[:, 1:]\\n\\n        log_probs = torch.log_softmax(shift_logits, dim=-1)\\n        probs = torch.exp(log_probs)\\n        token_entropy = -(probs * log_probs).sum(dim=-1)  # (B, T-1)\\n\\n        masked_entropy = (token_entropy * shift_mask).sum()\\n        total_entropy += masked_entropy.item()\\n        total_tokens += shift_mask.sum().item()\\n\\n    return total_entropy / max(total_tokens, 1)\\n\\n\\ndef compute_self_bleu(model, tokenizer, eval_dataset, gen_dataset=None,\\n                      max_samples=200, sample_pairs=500):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Self-BLEU among generated samples — higher means less diversity.\\n\\n    Uses `gen_dataset[\\\"text\\\"]`. Falls back to `eval_dataset` if gen_dataset\\n    is None (useful for baseline measurement on real data).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    src = gen_dataset if gen_dataset is not None else eval_dataset\\n    texts = src[\\\"text\\\"][:max_samples]\\n    tokenized = [tokenizer.tokenize(t)[:128] for t in texts if len(t.strip()) > 0]\\n\\n    if len(tokenized) < 2:\\n        return 0.0\\n\\n    smoother = SmoothingFunction().method1\\n    n_pairs = min(sample_pairs, len(tokenized) * (len(tokenized) - 1) // 2)\\n\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(0)\\n    indices = list(range(len(tokenized)))\\n    scores = []\\n    for _ in range(n_pairs):\\n        i, j = rng.choice(indices, size=2, replace=False)\\n        score = sentence_bleu([tokenized[i]], tokenized[j],\\n                              weights=(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25),\\n                              smoothing_function=smoother)\\n        scores.append(score)\\n\\n    return float(np.mean(scores))\\n\\n\\ndef compute_distinct_n(model, tokenizer, eval_dataset, gen_dataset=None,\\n                       max_samples=500, ns=(1, 2, 3)):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Distinct-n: ratio of unique n-grams to total n-grams in generated text.\\n\\n    Returns dict {\\\"distinct_1\\\": ..., \\\"distinct_2\\\": ..., \\\"distinct_3\\\": ...}.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    src = gen_dataset if gen_dataset is not None else eval_dataset\\n    texts = src[\\\"text\\\"][:max_samples]\\n    all_tokens = []\\n    for t in texts:\\n        all_tokens.extend(tokenizer.tokenize(t)[:256])\\n\\n    result = {}\\n    for n in ns:\\n        ngrams = [tuple(all_tokens[i:i + n]) for i in range(len(all_tokens) - n + 1)]\\n        if len(ngrams) == 0:\\n            result[f\\\"distinct_{n}\\\"] = 0.0\\n        else:\\n      \\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 742 字符] ...\\n\\ntext\\\"][:max_samples]\\n    for i in range(0, len(texts), batch_size):\\n        batch_texts = texts[i:i + batch_size]\\n        enc = tokenizer(batch_texts, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\", truncation=True,\\n                        max_length=max_length, padding=True)\\n        input_ids = enc[\\\"input_ids\\\"].to(device)\\n        attention_mask = enc[\\\"attention_mask\\\"].to(device)\\n\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            logits = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask).logits\\n\\n        shift_logits = logits[:, :-1, :]\\n        shift_labels = input_ids[:, 1:]\\n        shift_mask = attention_mask[:, 1:]\\n\\n        probs = torch.softmax(shift_logits, dim=-1)\\n        max_probs, preds = probs.max(dim=-1)\\n\\n        mask_flat = shift_mask.bool().flatten()\\n        confidences.append(max_probs.flatten()[mask_flat].cpu())\\n        accuracies.append((preds == shift_labels).flatten()[mask_flat].cpu().float())\\n\\n    confidences = torch.cat(confidences)\\n    accuracies = torch.cat(accuracies)\\n\\n    bin_boundaries = torch.linspace(0, 1, n_bins + 1)\\n    ece = 0.0\\n    for b in range(n_bins):\\n        mask = (confidences > bin_boundaries[b]) & (confidences <= bin_boundaries[b + 1])\\n        if mask.sum() == 0:\\n            continue\\n        bin_acc = accuracies[mask].mean()\\n        bin_conf = confidences[mask].mean()\\n        ece += mask.sum().item() / len(confidences) * abs(bin_acc - bin_conf).item()\\n\\n    return ece\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Spectral metrics\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef compute_effective_rank(model, tokenizer, eval_dataset, gen_dataset=None,\\n                           max_samples=256, max_length=256, batch_size=8):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Effective rank of last-layer hidden state covariance matrix.\\n\\n    effective_rank = exp(entropy of normalized eigenvalues)\\n    Declining effective rank signals representation collapse.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    model.eval()\\n    device = next(model.parameters()).device\\n    all_hidden = []\\n\\n    texts = eval_dataset[\\\"text\\\"][:max_samples]\\n    for i in range(0, len(texts), batch_size):\\n        batch_texts = texts[i:i + batch_size]\\n        enc = tokenizer(batch_texts, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\", truncation=True,\\n                        max_length=max_length, padding=True)\\n        input_ids = enc[\\\"input_ids\\\"].to(device)\\n        attention_mask = enc[\\\"attention_mask\\\"].to(device)\\n\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask,\\n                            output_hidden_states=True)\\n        last_hidden = outputs.hidden_states[-1]  # (B, T, D)\\n\\n        # take mean-pooled representation per sample\\n        mask_expanded = attention_mask.unsqueeze(-1).float()\\n        pooled = (last_hidden * mask_expanded).sum(dim=1) / mask_expanded.sum(dim=1)\\n        all_hidden.append(pooled.cpu().float())\\n\\n    H = torch.cat(all_hidden, dim=0).numpy()  # (N, D)\\n    if np.isnan(H).any() or np.isinf(H).any():\\n        return float('nan')\\n    H = H - H.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)\\n    cov = np.cov(H, rowvar=False)\\n    try:\\n        eigenvalues = np.linalg.eigvalsh(cov)\\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\\n        try:\\n            eigenvalues = np.linalg.svd(cov, compute_uv=False)\\n        except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\\n            return float('nan')\\n\\n    eigenvalues = eigenvalues[eigenvalues > 1e-12]\\n    if len(eigenvalues) == 0 or eigenvalues.sum() == 0:\\n        return float('nan')\\n\\n    # normalized eigenvalue distribution\\n    p = eigenvalues / eigenvalues.sum()\\n    entropy = -np.sum(p * np.log(p))\\n    return float(np.exp(entropy))\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Downstream metrics\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef compute_perplexity(model, tokenizer, eval_dataset, gen_dataset=None,\\n                       max_samples=512, max_length=256, batch_size=8):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Perplexity on held-out real data. The primary collapse indicator.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

```python
try:
    eigenvalues = np.linalg.eigvalsh(cov)
except np.linalg.LinAlgError:
    try:
        eigenvalues = np.linalg.svd(cov, compute_uv=False)
    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:
        return float('nan')
```

And there's also:
```python
eigenvalues = eigenvalues[eigenvalues > 1e-12]
if len(eigenvalues) == 0 or eigenvalues.sum() == 0:
    return float('nan')
```

And:
```python
if np.isnan(H).any() or np.isinf(H).any():
    return float('nan')
```

The implementation handles numerical stability well with a cascading fallback strategy: it tries eigenvalue decomposition first, falls back to SVD if that fails, and returns NaN as a last resort. It also filters out near-zero eigenvalues and validates the hidden states for NaN/Inf values before processing. Now I need to check how the compute_all_metrics wrapper applies this protection.

_source line 6203 · 2026-05-18T23:30:49.855Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The fix is clean and comprehensive:

1. **`compute_effective_rank`** (line 204-213): 
   - NaN/Inf check on hidden states
   - try/except eigvalsh → SVD fallback → NaN
   - Filter eigenvalues > 1e-12
   - Empty eigenvalue check

2. **`compute_all_metrics`** (line 298+):
   - All metric functions wrapped in individual try/except
   - Each failure returns NaN (or None for MAUVE)
   - Prints warning but doesn't terminate
   - `compute_distinct_n` handled separately since it returns a dict

This two-layer protection means the experiment won't crash even if effective_rank encounters numerical issues—it'll just log a warning and record NaN for that metric. I should let the Director know the fix looks solid and note that westd-17855 is now running at full GPU capacity with 6/6 active.

I'm also noticing that westd-19828 is still orphaned with the SmolLM3 bf16 process running under PID 395612.

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
This is concerning! Two experiments show `loss=0, grad_norm=nan`:

1. **a005_s42_fp32_v2** (cuda:3): gen_1 training, 37% done, loss=0, grad_norm=nan
2. **interv_pred_a075_s42_fp32_v2** (cuda:4): gen_0 training, 39% done, loss=0, grad_norm=nan

`loss=0, grad_norm=nan` is a serious training issue. This could mean:


- The loss computation is not working (no valid tokens contributing to loss)
- Numerical overflow/underflow in fp32
- Data loading issue (empty batches)
- Model weights became NaN

Wait - I'm realizing this is the same issue from the earlier diagnostic worker for interv_pred, where it noted "Gen 1 训练末期已出现 loss=0, grad_norm=nan（模型坍缩导致协方差矩阵病态）". The model collapses during training, weights become NaN, and then the covariance matrix becomes ill-conditioned, which is what triggered the LinAlgError initially. The metrics.py fix should catch this now.

But here's the thing - the metrics.py fix prevents the crash by returning NaN, but it doesn't address the root cause. The model itself is collapsing during training, which means the training output is garbage, any computed metrics will be NaN, the synthetic data generated will be garbage, and the whole experiment becomes meaningless.

For a005 with α=0.05, seeing loss=0 and grad_norm=nan at only 5% synthetic contamination is suspicious - this suggests there might be a bug in the training loop or data mixing itself, not just model collapse from the contamination. For interv_pred_a075 with α=0.75, the same pattern appears at gen_0 before any synthetic data is even introduced.

Actually, I'm realizing there's an edge case I need to check. At gen_0, there's no synthetic data from previous generations yet, so the model should be training on the original data. But the intervention setup specifies α=0.75, which would normally mix 75% synthetic with 25% real. If there's no synthetic data at gen_0, how does the mix_datasets() function handle that? I need to trace through the logic to see if there's an unhandled edge case there.

Looking at the worker report again, it shows gen_0 is actively training with loss=0 and grad_norm=nan, which is odd because gen_0 is typically just a baseline generation step without training. The fact that it's reporting training progress (1948/5000 batches) suggests either the setup is different than I expected, or there's something wrong with how the training is being initialized.

If gen_0 is indeed training with α=0.75, it would be trying to mix 75% synthetic data that doesn't exist yet, resulting in an empty or nearly empty training set—which would explain the loss=0 and nan gradients. This is a fundamental code issue in how the training loop handles the first generation, not something the metrics fix would address. For gen_1 with α=0.05, the synthetic data comes from gen_0's generation output, but if gen_0's model was already corrupted by broken training, the synthetic text it generates would be garbage, poisoning the gen_1 training set downstream.

The real concern here is that the loss=0/grad_norm=nan pattern is a symptom of broken training producing invalid models, and while the metrics.py fix prevents crashes, it's masking the underlying problem. I'm seeing a clear chain: training produces NaN weights → those weights corrupt the hidden states → the covariance becomes singular → metrics fail. The fix just swallows the metrics failure with SVD fallback, but the model itself is still broken. I need to check whether this NaN issue is specific to fp32 precision or if it's also happening in the bf16 experiments.

Actually, I'm realizing something important: these might be restarts from the original crashed runs. If the experiment crashed with a LinAlgError during gen_1 metrics, then restarted and loaded that corrupted gen_1 checkpoint (which already had NaN weights), it would continue training with NaN weights and produce exactly this loss=0/grad_norm=nan pattern. So the fix isn't just about metrics—it's about ensuring restarts load from a clean checkpoint like gen_0 or the original pretrained model, not from the crashed gen_1 state.
```

</details>

_source line 6272 · 2026-05-18T23:58:34.646Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Critical findings:

**a005_s42_fp32_v2 gen_1 metrics.json**:
- `token_entropy: NaN` ← metrics.py fix caught the error
- `self_bleu: 0.010` ← seems normal
- `ece: 0.0` ← suspicious, exactly 0.0
- `effective_rank: NaN` ← eigvalsh/SVD failure caught
- `perplexity: NaN` ← model weights are NaN, can't compute perplexity
- `distinct_1: 0.091` ← normal (text-surface metric, computed on generated text)


- `distinct_2: 0.502` ← normal
- `distinct_3: 0.832` ← normal
- `mauve: 0.029` ← normal

The root cause is clear: during gen_1 training on the mixed dataset (real + 5% synthetic), the loss collapsed to zero and gradients exploded into NaN, which corrupted the model weights and cascaded into NaN values for all weight-dependent metrics like perplexity, token entropy, and effective rank.

The text-surface metrics like distinct_n and mauve appear normal because they're computed from generated text rather than requiring forward passes through the broken model—they're likely using outputs from gen_0 instead. The model is essentially dead after gen_1 training, which is why the pipeline stalled.

This points to a deeper training stability issue in fp32 rather than just a metrics problem. I'm wondering why fp32 fails at gen_1 while bf16 succeeds—my initial thought is that the learning rate and batch size combination causes numerical instability in fp32, whereas bf16's stochastic rounding acts as implicit regularization. But I know there are successful fp32 experiments that completed, so I need to figure out what's different about those runs.

Looking back at my records, the α=0.00, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, and 1.00 experiments all finished successfully in fp32, likely on westd-19828. Now α=0.05 and α=0.60 are failing on westd-17855 with NaN gradients at gen_1. This could point to a server-specific issue—maybe westd-17855 and westd-19828 have different CUDA or driver versions that affect numerical precision.

The pattern is suspicious: the original crashes happened on westd-17855, and all successful runs were on westd-19828 (or earlier bf16 runs on westd-17855). Pythia-1.4B fp32 completed fine on westd-19828, and the Qwen3.5 fp32 on westd-17855 cuda:1 hasn't reached the metrics step yet. I need to check whether the earlier α=0.05 and α=0.60 runs were using the same configuration.

The real issue might be in the gen_0 training flow itself. The interv_pred_a075_s42_fp32 experiment shows loss=0 and grad_norm=nan at generation 0, which suggests something's wrong with how the synthetic data mixing works when there's no previous generation to draw from. At gen_0, the code tries to create a 75% synthetic mix, but there's no synthetic data yet—only the pre-trained model's output. I need to trace through what actually happens in that scenario. The pre-trained model generates reasonable quality text, but both experiments are hitting NaN losses during training at different generations—interv_pred at gen_0 and a005 at gen_1. Since these are fresh restarts, the issue isn't corrupted checkpoints. Looking at a005's gen_1 metrics, the distinct_1 value is normal but token_entropy shows NaN, suggesting the model's weights diverged during training even though it completed and computed metrics afterward.

I think the metrics.json for gen_1 actually represents the pre-training state—text-based metrics like distinct and mauve were computed on previously generated text, while the model-based metrics that are now NaN would have been computed after training failed. This suggests the pipeline generates synthetic data first, then trains the model, and the NaN gradients are causing the weights to explode partway through.

If gen_0 training already corrupted the model with NaN weights, then gen_1 is inheriting a broken model from the start, which explains why it immediately shows loss=0 and NaN gradients. But a005 uses only 5% synthetic data mixed with 95% real data during gen_0 training—that should be stable enough for fp32. Let me look at what actually happened in gen_0's metrics to understand where the divergence started.

- interv_pred gen_0: identical values ✅ normal baseline

Now looking at a005's gen_1 metrics, I see NaN values across token_entropy, perplexity, and eff_rank, with ece=0.0 indicating the model collapsed during training. The distinct and mauve scores are still normal since they're computed from generated text rather than model outputs. So the model stayed intact through gen_0 but the weights degraded to NaN somewhere during gen_1 training. The puzzle is why this NaN collapse happens on westd-17855 but not on westd-19828, especially since all the successful fp32 experiments with varying α values seem to avoid this issue.

I'm wondering if this is a server-specific problem—different PyTorch or CUDA versions, GPU firmware differences, or even hardware issues like thermal throttling causing bit errors. But let me actually verify whether all the successful runs really were on westd-19828 or if some predate the server split entirely.

Now I'm noticing that every new experiment on westd-17855 crashes with the same LinAlgError pattern leading to zero or NaN loss, which feels too consistent to be random. I need to check whether the other fp32 run on cuda:2 also hit this issue or if it's still in a different phase of execution.

_source line 6289 · 2026-05-18T23:59:24.332Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
CONFIRMED: a060_v2 gen_1 metrics also show `token_entropy: NaN, perplexity: NaN, effective_rank: NaN, ece: 0.0` — the exact same pattern as a005_v2.

All THREE fp32 experiments restarted on westd-17855 have the same problem:
1. gen_0 baseline: normal (pre-trained model)
2. gen_0 training → model weights become NaN
3. gen_1 metrics: NaN for model-based metrics

And critically: the distinct_1 values in gen_1 metrics for all three are IDENTICAL:


- a005 and a060 have exactly matching distinct_1 and mauve values, which means they're computing metrics on the same generated text despite being separate experiments.

- This makes sense if gen_0 uses the same pre-trained model and parameters in both cases, producing identical text before any training happens.

- But I'm realizing there's a timing issue here — if gen_1 metrics are computed after gen_0 training corrupted the weights with NaN values, the generated text should be garbage, not identical to gen_0. Unless these metrics are actually from the post-gen_0 checkpoint before gen_1 generation even starts, which would explain why the text-based metrics still match.

The real pattern emerging is that fp32 training on westd-17855 consistently produces NaN weights during gen_0 across all three α values (0.05, 0.60, 0.75), while the same setup works fine on westd-19828. This suggests either a server-specific hardware or software issue causing numerical instability, or the experiments were actually submitted with different configurations between the two machines. I should investigate whether there are differences in PyTorch or numpy versions that could explain this divergence.
```

Found it!

- **westd-17855**: PyTorch **2.8.0+cu128**
- **westd-19828**: PyTorch **2.10.0+cu128**

Major version difference! PyTorch 2.8.0 vs 2.10.0. This is a significant difference. Between these versions, there could be changes in:
- fp32 training behavior
- Optimizer implementations (AdamW)
- Loss computation
- Gradient computation
- Memory management
- Autograd behavior

This could easily explain why fp32 training produces NaN on westd-17855 (PyTorch 2.8.0) but works fine on westd-19828 (PyTorch 2.10.0). 

A bug fix or behavior change between 2.8 and 2.10 is almost certainly causing the numerical instability in fp32 training on the older version. The metrics.py fix was just papering over the symptoms — the real problem is the PyTorch version mismatch. I need to either upgrade PyTorch on westd-17855, run all fp32 experiments on westd-19828, or dig into what specifically changed in PyTorch 2.8.0 that breaks fp32 training. This is the actual root cause.

```text
Great data. Let me analyze the Pythia-1.4B α=0.50 fp32 results:

**Key metrics trajectory (gen 0 → gen 10):**

| Metric | Gen 0 | Gen 1 | Gen 5 | Gen 10 | Drift % |
|--------|-------|-------|-------|--------|---------|
| token_entropy | 2.946 | 2.134 | 2.159 | 2.227 | -24.4% |
| perplexity | 19.68 | 20.71 | 30.63 | 35.14 | +78.6% |


| distinct_1 | 0.0993 | 0.0998 | 0.0686 | 0.0636 | -36.0% |
| distinct_2 | 0.521 | 0.520 | 0.313 | 0.262 | -49.7% |
| distinct_3 | 0.813 | 0.807 | 0.508 | 0.408 | -49.8% |
| ece | 0.0071 | 0.104 |

The diversity metrics show a sharp collapse across all n-gram levels, with distinct_2 and distinct_3 dropping by roughly half, while calibration error spikes dramatically after generation 1.

Looking at the effective rank and other metrics, I'm seeing entropy plummets at the first generation then plateaus, which differs from the Pythia-410M pattern where it continued declining. The diversity degradation follows a similar trajectory though—the n-gram distinctness drops are nearly identical to what I observed at α=0.50.

The perplexity increase is moderate at 78.6%, sitting between the baseline and catastrophic collapse scenarios. The ECE spike at generation one is sharp and dramatic, jumping 14.6×, which serves as a clear warning signal before gradually worsening. What's striking is the effective rank actually climbing by 62%—this shouldn't happen during a collapse, suggesting the model might be spreading its representations across a wider space even as it loses semantic diversity, or this could be a quirk of the larger model architecture.

The lead-lag pattern holds up at this scale: entropy and ECE both spike at generation one as canary signals, while diversity stays stable initially and only begins declining at generation two. This one-generation offset between the early warning signs and actual diversity loss aligns with what I'd expect. Comparing to the smaller 410M model, the 1.4B version shows more dramatic entropy and perplexity shifts despite similar diversity drops, suggesting larger models experience sharper metric swings during degradation. Now I'm going to write up the findings for the paper.

_source line 6369 · 2026-05-19T00:02:27.970Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Excellent! The worker found the REAL root cause. It's NOT a PyTorch version issue (though that might also be a factor). The actual root cause is:

**When `--bf16` is not set, the model loads in its native dtype (float16 for Pythia-410M), but the Trainer doesn't enable AMP/GradScaler. This causes gradient overflow in fp16 → all weights become NaN.**

Specifically:
- `bf16=False` → `torch_dtype=None` → model loads in native dtype (float16 for Pythia)


- Without AMP enabled, AdamW runs in fp16 without loss scaling, causing gradients to overflow after warmup and parameters to become NaN around step 900

The fix is to explicitly set the dtype to float32 when bf16 isn't enabled, which explains why the fp32 experiments in westd-19828 worked—they were likely loading models with proper dtype handling from the start.

Wait, I'm realizing the original westd-19828 experiments ran before the D079 code changes. The old code actually set `fp16 = (not args.bf16) and torch.cuda.is_available()`, meaning when bf16 was False, fp16 would automatically be True, which would have enabled proper loss scaling through AMP.

The D079 change forced `fp16=False` to achieve "pure fp32" training, but it created a critical mismatch: the model still loads in its native fp16 dtype while the trainer no longer applies loss scaling. The pre-D079 experiments succeeded because they had both the fp16 model AND the AMP/GradScaler mechanism to prevent gradient overflow — the D079 change inadvertently broke this by removing the loss scaling while keeping the fp16 model.

Now I'm realizing the westd-19828 experiments that worked might have succeeded for a different reason. The scale_14b experiment uses Pythia-1.4B, which likely has a native float32 dtype rather than float16. So when `torch_dtype=None` loads it, it stays in float32, and training works without needing AMP. But Pythia-410M's native dtype is confirmed to be float16, which is why it hits the gradient overflow issue.

The pattern is clear: D079 introduced a dtype mismatch bug where models with native float16 weights get trained in float16 without AMP, causing instability. The fix is straightforward — explicitly set `torch.float32` when bf16 is disabled, which would have prevented this entire issue in the first place.
```

</details>

  "text": "[severity: critical] **更正：westd-17855 fp32 NaN 的真正根因是 D079 代码 bug，非 PyTorch 版本差异**\n\n撤回我之前关于 PyTorch 2.8 vs 2.10 的假说。Worker 诊断找到了真正根因：\n\n**Bug**: `iterative_train.py` L218 — `bf16=False` 时传入 `torch_dtype=None`，Pythia-410M 原生 dtype 为 float16，模型以 fp16 加载。但 D079 改动将 `TrainingArguments.fp16` 从 `True` 改为 `False`，导致 Trainer 不启用 AMP/GradScaler。**fp16 模型 + 无 loss scaling = warmup 后梯度溢出 → 全模型 NaN。**\n\n**这解释了所有观察：**\n1. Pre-D079 实验正常：`fp16=True` → Trainer 启用 AMP + GradScaler → fp16 安全训练\n2. Post-D079 Pythia-410M 崩溃：`fp16=False` + 模型仍 fp16 → 无 GradScaler → 梯度溢出\n3. Pythia-1.4B 正常：原生 dtype 可能是 float32，`torch_dtype=None` 加载为 fp32\n4. bf16 实验不受影响：`torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16` 显式设定，Trainer 启用 bf16 AMP\n5. 崩溃发生在 ~step 900（warmup 结束后 lr 达峰值），100% 可复现\n\n**修复**（一行改动，两处）：\n```python\n# torch_dtype=None → torch_dtype=torch.float32\nmodel = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n    args.model_name, \n    **{_DTYPE_KWARG: torch.bfloat16 if args.bf16 else torch.float32})\n```\n\n修复后需清除失败的 gen_1 目录并重跑 a005/a060/interv_pred_a075。cuda:3 和 cuda:4 已自动释放。",
  "project": "distrib_canary_collapse"
}
````

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
This is a005 v3 resubmission. But the critical question is: **Did the worker apply the dtype fix?** The previous worker identified the root cause as `torch_dtype=None` causing Pythia-410M to load as fp16. The fix needs `torch_dtype=torch.float32` when bf16 is False.

If the worker just resubmitted without fixing `iterative_train.py`, the experiment will crash again at ~step 900 with the same NaN gradient issue.

Let me check if the code was actually fixed.
```
